8.8s 573 GPU_NAME_PATTERNS = {'rtx-pro-6000': ('rtx pro 6000',), 'h100': ('h100',), 'l4': ('l4',)}
8.8s 574 
8.8s 575 
8.8s 576 def taaf_kaggle_input_paths() -> dict[str, Path]:
8.8s 577 raw = os.getenv('TAAF_KAGGLE_INPUT_PATHS', '').strip()
8.8s 578 if not raw:
8.8s 579 return {}
8.8s 580 data = json.loads(raw)
8.8s 581 if not isinstance(data, dict):
8.8s 582 raise RuntimeError('TAAF_KAGGLE_INPUT_PATHS must contain a JSON object.')
8.8s 583 return {str(ref): Path(str(path)) for ref, path in data.items()}
8.8s 584 
8.8s 585 
8.8s 586 def resolve_kaggle_dataset_path(owner: str, slug: str) -> Path:
8.8s 587 mapped = taaf_kaggle_input_paths().get(f'{owner}/{slug}')
8.8s 588 if mapped is not None:
8.8s 589 return mapped
8.8s 590 for dataset_path in (Path('/kaggle/input') / slug, Path('/kaggle/input/datasets') / owner / slug):
8.8s 591 if dataset_path.exists():
8.8s 592 return dataset_path
8.8s 593 return Path('/kaggle/input') / slug
8.8s 594 
8.8s 595 
8.8s 596 WHEELHOUSE = resolve_kaggle_dataset_path(WHEELHOUSE_OWNER, WHEELHOUSE_SLUG)
8.8s 597 MODEL_PATH = resolve_kaggle_dataset_path(MODEL_OWNER, MODEL_SLUG)

8.8s 599 
8.8s 600 def assert_expected_cuda_gpu() -> None:
8.8s 601 if not Path('/kaggle/input').exists():
8.8s 602 return
8.8s 603 assert shutil.which('nvidia-smi'), 'CUDA GPU check failed: nvidia-smi is not available.'
8.8s 604 result = subprocess.run(['nvidia-smi', '--query-gpu=name', '--format=csv,noheader'], capture_output=True, text=True)
8.8s 605 assert result.returncode == 0, f'nvidia-smi failed: {result.stderr.strip()}'
8.8s 606 gpu_names = [line.strip() for line in result.stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()]
8.8s 607 assert gpu_names, 'nvidia-smi did not report any CUDA GPUs.'
8.8s 608 expected_gpu_type = os.getenv('KAGGLE_GPU_TYPE', 'rtx-pro-6000').strip().lower()
8.8s 609 expected_count = os.getenv('KAGGLE_GPU_COUNT', '1')
8.8s 610 if expected_count.isdigit():
8.8s 611 assert len(gpu_names) == int(expected_count), f'Expected {expected_count} CUDA GPU(s), found {gpu_names}'
8.8s 612 patterns = GPU_NAME_PATTERNS.get(expected_gpu_type, (expected_gpu_type.replace('-', ' '),))
8.8s 613 mismatched = [name for name in gpu_names if not any(pattern in name.lower() for pattern in patterns)]
8.8s 614 assert not mismatched, f'Expected GPU type {expected_gpu_type!r}, found {gpu_names}'
8.8s 615 print(f'CUDA GPU check passed for {expected_gpu_type} x{expected_count}: {gpu_names}', flush=True)
8.8s 616 
8.8s 617 
8.8s 618 def vllm_env() -> dict[str, str]:
8.8s 619 env = os.environ.copy()
8.8s 620 existing = env.get('PYTHONPATH', '')
8.8s 621 env['PYTHONPATH'] = str(SITE_PACKAGES) if not existing else f'{SITE_PACKAGES}{os.pathsep}{existing}'
8.8s 622 env.update(
8.8s 623 {
8.8s 624 'USE_TF': '0',
8.8s 625 'TRANSFORMERS_NO_TF': '1',
8.8s 626 'TRANSFORMERS_NO_TORCHVISION': '1',
8.8s 627 'VLLM_NO_USAGE_STATS': '1',
8.8s 628 'HF_HUB_OFFLINE': '1',
8.8s 629 'TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE': '1',
8.8s 630 }
8.8s 631 )
8.8s 632 return env

8.8s 634 
8.8s 635 def cached_install_is_usable() -> bool:
8.8s 636 if not INSTALL_STAMP.exists() or INSTALL_STAMP.read_text(encoding='utf-8') != STAMP_TEXT:
8.8s 637 return False
8.8s 638 result = subprocess.run(
8.8s 639 [sys.executable, '-c', "import vllm, torch; print(f'Cached vLLM {vllm.__version__}, torch {torch.__version__}')"],
8.8s 640 env=vllm_env(),
8.8s 641 text=True,
8.8s 642 )
8.8s 643 return result.returncode == 0
8.8s 644 
8.8s 645 
8.8s 646 def install_vllm_wheelhouse() -> None:
8.8s 647 requirements = WHEELHOUSE / 'requirements.lock'
8.8s 648 if not requirements.exists():
8.8s 649 raise FileNotFoundError(f'Missing wheelhouse lock file: {requirements}')
8.8s 650 if cached_install_is_usable():
8.8s 651 print(f'Using cached vLLM target install at {SITE_PACKAGES}', flush=True)
8.8s 652 return
8.8s 653 shutil.rmtree(SITE_PACKAGES, ignore_errors=True)
8.8s 654 SITE_PACKAGES.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
8.8s 655 cmd = [
8.8s 656 sys.executable,
8.8s 657 '-m',
8.8s 658 'pip',
8.8s 659 'install',
8.8s 660 '--no-index',
8.8s 661 '--find-links',
8.8s 662 str(WHEELHOUSE),
8.8s 663 '--requirement',
8.8s 664 str(requirements),
8.8s 665 '--target',
8.8s 666 str(SITE_PACKAGES),
8.8s 667 '--upgrade',
8.8s 668 '--ignore-installed',
8.8s 669 '--only-binary',
8.8s 670 ':all:',
8.8s 671 '--no-compile',
8.8s 672 '--disable-pip-version-check',
8.8s 673 '--no-warn-conflicts',
8.8s 674 ]
8.8s 675 print('Installing vLLM wheelhouse into', SITE_PACKAGES, flush=True)
8.8s 676 subprocess.run(cmd, check=True)
8.8s 677 INSTALL_STAMP.write_text(STAMP_TEXT, encoding='utf-8')
8.8s 678 
8.8s 679 
8.8s 680 def request_json(url: str, payload: dict | None = None, timeout: int = 30) -> dict:
8.8s 681 data = None if payload is None else json.dumps(payload).encode('utf-8')
8.8s 682 request = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
8.8s 683 with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
8.8s 684 return json.loads(response.read().decode('utf-8'))
8.8s 685 
8.8s 686 
8.8s 687 def tail_server_log(lines: int = 80) -> str:
8.8s 688 if not VLLM_SERVER_LOG.exists():
8.8s 689 return ''
8.8s 690 return '\n'.join(VLLM_SERVER_LOG.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').splitlines()[-lines:])
8.8s 691 
8.8s 692 
8.8s 693 def wait_for_vllm_server(timeout_seconds: int = 900) -> None:
8.8s 694 deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_seconds
8.8s 695 url = f'{VLLM_BASE_URL}/models'
8.8s 696 while time.monotonic() < deadline:
8.8s 697 if VLLM_SERVER_PID.exists():
8.8s 698 try:
8.8s 699 os.kill(int(VLLM_SERVER_PID.read_text().strip()), 0)
8.8s 700 except OSError as exc:
8.8s 701 raise RuntimeError(f'vLLM server process is not alive: {exc}\n{tail_server_log()}') from exc
8.8s 702 try:
8.8s 703 models = request_json(url, timeout=5)
8.8s 704 print('vLLM server ready:', models, flush=True)
8.8s 705 return
8.8s 706 except Exception:
8.8s 707 time.sleep(5)
8.8s 708 raise TimeoutError(f'Timed out waiting for vLLM server at {url}.\nLast server log lines:\n{tail_server_log()}')
8.8s 709 
8.8s 710 
8.8s 711 def start_vllm_server() -> None:
8.8s 712 install_vllm_wheelhouse()
8.8s 713 VLLM_SERVER_LOG.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
8.8s 714 VLLM_SERVER_PID.unlink(missing_ok=True)
8.8s 715 log_handle = VLLM_SERVER_LOG.open('w', encoding='utf-8')
8.8s 716 cmd = [
8.8s 717 sys.executable,
8.8s 718 '-m',
8.8s 719 'vllm.entrypoints.openai.api_server',
8.8s 720 '--model',
8.8s 721 str(MODEL_PATH),
8.8s 722 '--served-model-name',
8.8s 723 SERVED_MODEL_NAME,
8.8s 724 '--host',
8.8s 725 VLLM_HOST,
8.8s 726 '--port',
8.8s 727 str(VLLM_PORT),
8.8s 728 '--tensor-parallel-size',
8.8s 729 str(VLLM_TENSOR_PARALLEL_SIZE),
8.8s 730 '--enable-auto-tool-choice',
8.8s 731 '--tool-call-parser',
8.8s 732 'qwen3_coder',
8.8s 733 '--generation-config',
8.8s 734 'vllm',
8.8s 735 '--enable-prefix-caching',
8.8s 736 '--default-chat-template-kwargs',
8.8s 737 '{"preserve_thinking": true}',
8.8s 738 '--reasoning-parser',
8.8s 739 'qwen3',
8.8s 740 '--max-model-len',
8.8s 741 str(VLLM_MAX_MODEL_LEN),
8.8s 742 ]
8.8s 743 print('Starting vLLM OpenAI server:', ' '.join(cmd), flush=True)
8.8s 744 process = subprocess.Popen(cmd, env=vllm_env(), stdout=log_handle, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)
8.8s 745 VLLM_SERVER_PID.write_text(str(process.pid), encoding='utf-8')
8.8s 746 wait_for_vllm_server()
8.8s 747

8.8s 749 def run_vllm_api_smoke_test() -> None:
8.8s 750 payload = {
8.8s 751 'model': SERVED_MODEL_NAME,
8.8s 752 'messages': [{'role': 'user', 'content': 'Answer in one short sentence: what is 2 + 2?'}],
8.8s 753 'temperature': 0.0,
8.8s 754 'max_tokens': 96,
8.8s 755 'chat_template_kwargs': {'enable_thinking': False},
8.8s 756 }

8.8s 758 generated = response['choices'][0]['message'].get('content', '').strip()
8.8s 759 print('\n' + '=' * 88, flush=True)
8.8s 760 print('VLLM OPENAI SERVER QWEN SMOKE TEST REAL MODEL OUTPUT', flush=True)

8.8s 762 print('=' * 88 + '\n', flush=True)
